function calculateLayout(gridWidth, gridHeight, containerWidth, containerHeight) {\n  const padding = 16;\n  const avail = Math.min(containerWidth, containerHeight) - padding * 2;\n  const maxDim = Math.max(gridWidth, gridHeight);\n  const cellSize = Math.floor((avail - CELL_GAP * (maxDim - 1)) / maxDim);\n\n  const gridW = gridWidth * cellSize + (gridWidth - 1) * CELL_GAP;\n  const gridH = };\n}\n\n/**\n * Convert canvas coordinates to cell - pure hit-testing\n */\nexport function canvasToCell(canvasX, canvasY, gridWidth, gridHeight, cellSize, offsetX, offsetY) {\n  for (let r = 0; r < gridHeight; r++) {\n    for (let c = 0; c < gridWidth; c++) {\n      const pos = getCellPosition(r, c, cellSize, offsetX, offsetY);\n      if (canvasX >= pos.x && canvasX <= pos.x + cellSize &&\n          canvasY >= pos.y && canvasY <= pos.y + cellSize) {\n        return { r, c };\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Merge Games Phaser Scene\n */\nclass MergeGamesScene extends this.dragGraphics.setVisible(false);\n        this.renderCells();\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const dropCell = canvasToCell(\n        pointer.x, pointer.y,\n        this.state.width,   }\n\n    graphics.setAlpha(alpha);\n\n    // Shadow\n    graphics.fillStyle(0x000000, 0.22);\n    graphics.fillRoundedRect(0, 3, cellSize, cellSize, CELL_RADIUS);\n\n    // Tier glow for higher y: 16 + row * (TUBE_HEIGHT + TUBE_GAP) * tubeScale\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Convert canvas coordinates to tube index - pure hit-testing\n */\nexport function canvasToTubeIndex(canvasX, canvasY, tubeCount, graphics.lineTo(x + width - tr, y);\n    graphics.arc(x + width - tr, y + tr, tr, -Math.PI / 2, 0, false);\n    graphics.lineTo(x + width, y + height - br);\n    graphics.arc(x + width - br, y + graphics.arc(x + tl, y + tl, tl, Math.PI, -Math.PI / 2, false);\n    graphics.closePath();\n  }\n\n  setState(newState) {\n    this.state = newState;\n    this.updateLayout();\n this.pourGraphics.strokePath();\n          }\n\n          // Redraw tubes to show segment changes during animation\n          this.drawTube(fromIdx);\n          this.drawTube(toIdx);\n        },\n 'horizontal') {\n      if (row === v.y && col >= v.x && col < v.x + v.width) return v.id;\n    } else {\n      if (col === v.x && row >= v.y && row < v.y + v.height) return v.id;\n    }\n  }\n  return 'right' : 'left', distance: Math.abs(cells) };\n  } else {\n    const cells = Math.round(dy / cellSize);\n    if (cells === 0) return null;\n    return { direction: cells > 0 ? = Math.min(255, (n >> 16) + a);\n  const G = Math.min(255, ((n >> 8) & 0xff) + a);\n  const B = Math.min(255, (n & 0xff) + a);\n  return `#${(0x1000000 + R * 0x10000 + G * 0x100 + B).toString(16).slice(1)}`;\n}\n\nfunction darken(hex, pct) {\n  const n = parseInt(hex.replace('#', ''), 16);\n  const a = Math.round(2.55 * pct);\n  const R = Math.max(0, (n >> 16) - a);\n  const G offsetY + r * cellSize + ((seed * 13) % 31) / 31 * cellSize;\n        this.gridGraphics.fillCircle(sx, sy, 1.5);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Parking space lines\n    this.gridGraphics.lineStyle(1.5, = cellSize * 0.2;\n        this.gridGraphics.beginPath();\n        this.gridGraphics.moveTo(x, y);\n        this.gridGraphics.lineTo(x, Math.min(y + dashLen, offsetY + gridPxH - 4));\n        this.gridGraphics.strokePath();\n      }\n    }\n    for (let r = 1; r < gh; r++) {\n      const y = offsetY + r * cellSize;\n      for (let x = offsetX + 4; x < offsetX + gridPxW - 4; x += cellSize * 'hero';\n\n    // Side face (shadow)\n    const sideColor = Phaser.Display.Color.HexStringToColor(darken(v.color, 30)).color;\n    graphics.fillStyle(sideColor, 1);\n graphics.fillStyle(topColor, 1);\n    graphics.fillRoundedRect(0, 0, pw, ph, r);\n\n    // Gradient overlay (top highlight)\n    graphics.fillStyle(lightColor, 0.4);\n    graphics.fillRoundedRect(0, 0.28);\n    graphics.fillRoundedRect(4, 3, pw - 8, ph * 0.22, r * 0.5);\n\n    // Windows\n    graphics.fillStyle(0xc8f0ff, 0.35);\n    if (v.orientation === 'horizontal') {\n      const ww = pw * 0.52;\n      const wh = ph * 0.14;\n      const wx = pw * 0.24;\n      graphics.fillRect(wx, ph * 0.1, ww, wh);\n      if (v.height >= 3) graphics.fillRect(wx, ph * 0.45, ww, wh);\n    }\n\n    // Windshield glare\n    graphics.fillStyle(0xffffff, 0.12);\n    if (v.orientation === 'horizontal') {\n      graphics.beginPath();\n      graphics.moveTo(pw * 0.1, ph * 0.2);\n      graphics.lineTo(pw to top\n      if (v.type === 'hero') {\n        container.setDepth(10);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  setupInput() {\n    this.input.on('pointerdown', (pointer) => {\n      if (this.state.status !== setReducedMotion(value) {\n    this.reducedMotion = value;\n  }\n\n  setHintVehicle(vehicleId) {\n    // Clear existing hint tweens\n    this.hintTweens.forEach(tween => tween.stop());\n 0 }, { won: true });\n    const moves = this.state.moves;\n    const level = this.levels[this.currentLevelIndex];\n    const stars = moves <= 5 ? 3 : moves <= 10 ? 2 : 1;\n    await updateGameStats(GAME_ID, { lastLevel: this.currentLevelIndex, played: 1, completed: 1, stars });\n    await awardLevelComplete(GAME_ID, stars, { levelId: this.currentLevelIndex, moves });\n    document.getElementById('stars-display').querySelectorAll('.star').forEach((el, i) => {\n      el.classList.toggle('filled', i < stars);\n    });\n    document.getElementById('stats-summary').textContent = `Reached Tier ${level.task.targetTier} in ${moves} merge${moves !== 1 ? 's' : ''}!`;\n    this.winOverlay.classList.add('active');\n    this.winOverlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');\n    announce(`Level complete! Reached Tier ${level.task.targetTier} in ${moves} merge${moves !== 1 ? 's' : ''}. ${stars} star${stars !== 1 ? 's' : ''}!`);\n  }\n\n  handleResize() {\n    if (this.state && this.renderer) { this.renderer.resize(this.state); this.render(); }\n  }\n\n  render() {\n    if (this.state && this.renderer) this.renderer.render(this.state, null);\n  }\n\n  updateUI() {\n    if (!this.state) return;\n    const level = this.levels[this.currentLevelIndex];\n    this.levelDisplay.textContent = this.currentLevelIndex + 1;\n    this.movesDisplay.textContent = this.state.moves;\n    this.goalDisplay.textContent = `T${level.task.targetTier}×${level.task.targetCount}`;\n    this.levelProgress.textContent = `Level ${this.currentLevelIndex + 1} / ${this.levels.length}`;\n    this.taskText.textContent = `Merge tiles to reach Tier ${level.task.targetTier}` +\n      (level.task.targetCount > 1 ? ` × ${level.task.targetCount}` : '');\n    this.btnPrev.disabled = this.currentLevelIndex === 0;\n    this.btnNext.disabled = this.currentLevelIndex >= this.levels.length - 1;\n  }\n\n  showSettings() {\n    const s = getSettings();\n    document.getElementById('setting-sound').checked = s.soundEnabled;\n    document.getElementById('setting-motion').checked = s.reducedMotion;\n    this.settingsOverlay.classList.add('active');\n    this.settingsOverlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');\n  }\n}\n\ndocument.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n  const game = new MergeGame();\n  game.init();\n});\n\nexport { MergeGame };\nexport default MergeGame;\n","numLines":225,"startLine":1,"totalLines":225}}}
